# ARENA Corpus Glossary v3

Sub-categorised + corpus-fingerprinted

**Coverage.** 760 glossary entries.

**How to read fingerprint distinctiveness.** A category at "(65% / 7.9×)" means: 65% of this term's mentions fall in that category, which is 7.9× the rate the same category appears in the broader corpus. Values >2× indicate the term is a marker for that cohort. Values near 1.0× indicate general-vocabulary.

## technology

### (uncategorised)  (231)

#### AC — *Alternating Current*

**technology** · 2,085 mentions / 282 docs

Electric current that periodically reverses direction, as used in power grids worldwide. In Australia the standard is 50 Hz. AC is the dominant transmission and distribution standard; inverters convert DC (from solar PV or batteries) to AC for grid injection.

*Context (model):* Appears in solar, storage, and grid integration project documentation when describing system architecture, inverter specifications, and grid connection requirements.

*Fingerprint* (2,077 mentions / 282 docs):
  - trajectory: **steady (median 2019)** 2010–2025
  - category concentration: Battery storage (18% / 2.1×), Solar energy (19% / 0.9×), Solar PV R&amp;D (10% / 0.8×)
  - top projects: Co-located Vanadium Flow Battery Storage and Solar (126), Renewable Green Hydrogen Feasibility (102), NT Solar Energy Transformation Program (95)

#### ACFA — *Advanced Control and Forecasting Algorithm*

**technology** · 613 mentions / 10 docs

A control system used with thermal energy storage that uses price forecasts and load predictions to optimise TES charge/discharge scheduling for cost reduction and demand response.

*Fingerprint* (598 mentions / 10 docs):
  - trajectory: **steady (median 2019)** 2016–2019
  - category concentration: Renewables for industry (95% / 17.5×)
  - lead orgs: Glaciem Cooling Technologies Pty Ltd (98% / 149.8×)
  - top projects: Advancing Renewables with PCM Thermal Energy Storage (581), Maximising Solar PV with Phase Change Thermal Energy Storage (10)

*Notes:* v1 flagged as noise/uncertain; corpus clearly expands as 'Advanced Control and Forecasting Algorithm' in the Glaciem/Reef HQ TES project context.

#### ADMS — *Advanced Distribution Management System*

**technology** · 277 mentions / 22 docs

A software platform that integrates distribution network management functions including SCADA, outage management, volt-VAR optimisation, DER management, and network modelling to enable real-time, optimised operation of distribution networks with high DER penetration.

*Context (model):* Appears in ARENA network management, DSO, and DER integration project documentation as the enabling technology for intelligent distribution grid operation.

*Fingerprint* (268 mentions / 22 docs):
  - trajectory: **steady (median 2019)** 2016–2025
  - category concentration: Distributed energy resources (92% / 11.6×)
  - lead orgs: Zeppelin Bend Pty Ltd (68% / 166.3×)
  - top projects: evolve DER Project (176), DER Integration and Automation Project (25), Western Australia Distributed Energy Resources Orchestration Pilot (Project Symphony) (16)

#### AE — *Alkaline Electrolyser*

**technology** · 178 mentions / 11 docs

An electrolyser using an alkaline electrolyte to split water into hydrogen and oxygen. Alkaline electrolysers are a mature, cost-competitive technology for large-scale green hydrogen production.

*Context (model):* Appears in ARENA green hydrogen project documentation comparing electrolyser technologies; often used interchangeably with 'AEC'.

*Fingerprint* (178 mentions / 11 docs):
  - trajectory: **rising (median 2021 vs corpus 2019)** 2012–2021
  - category concentration: Renewables for industry (38% / 7.0×), Distributed energy resources (40% / 5.1×)
  - lead orgs: Monash University (ClimateWorks Australia) (41% / 126.2×), Electricity Networks Corporation (Western Power) (40% / 24.5×)
  - top projects: Western Australia Distributed Energy Resources Orchestration Pilot (Project Symphony) (63), Industry ETI Establishment Phase (48), Australian Industry ETI Delivery Stage Project (17)

#### AEC — *Alkaline Electrolysis Cell*

**technology** · 186 mentions / 24 docs

A mature electrolyser technology that uses a liquid alkaline electrolyte (typically potassium hydroxide solution) to split water into hydrogen and oxygen. AEC is a proven, lower-cost but less dynamic technology than PEM electrolysis.

*Context (model):* Appears in ARENA green hydrogen project documentation comparing electrolyser technology options.

*Fingerprint* (176 mentions / 24 docs):
  - trajectory: **rising (median 2021 vs corpus 2019)** 2017–2023
  - category concentration: System security and reliability (73% / 18.8×)
  - lead orgs: Australian Energy Council (75% / 229.1×)
  - top projects: Australian Energy Council Double-Sided Causer Pays Study (118), Hornsdale Power Reserve Upgrade (10), SA Power Networks Market Active Solar Trial (6)

*Notes:* Could also refer to 'Australian Energy Council' (the electricity generator and retailer peak body). Context distinguishes the two.

#### AGC — *Automatic Generation Control*

**technology** · 809 mentions / 43 docs

A control system used by power system operators to automatically adjust the output of generators to maintain system frequency and meet scheduled inter-regional power flows. AGC operates in the regulation timescale (seconds to minutes).

*Context (model):* Referenced in ARENA grid stability, storage, and frequency regulation projects examining automated dispatch and FCAS regulation services.

*Fingerprint* (761 mentions / 43 docs):
  - trajectory: **rising (median 2021 vs corpus 2019)** 2017–2022
  - category concentration: System security and reliability (63% / 16.3×), Wind energy (17% / 5.5×), Battery storage (14% / 1.6×)
  - lead orgs: Australian Energy Council (55% / 169.5×), Hornsdale Wind Farm 2 Pty Ltd (15% / 46.5×), Infigen Energy (11% / 28.1×)
  - top projects: Australian Energy Council Double-Sided Causer Pays Study (405), Hornsdale Wind Farm Stage 2 FCAS Trial (111), Lake Bonney Battery Energy Storage System (84)

#### ALD — *Atomic Layer Deposition*

**technology** · 310 mentions / 23 docs

A thin-film deposition technique that deposits material one atomic layer at a time using sequential, self-limiting chemical reactions. ALD is used to create ultra-thin passivation and contact layers in high-efficiency solar cells.

*Context (model):* Appears in ARENA solar cell fabrication and materials research project documentation.

*Fingerprint* (307 mentions / 23 docs):
  - trajectory: **falling (median 2012 vs corpus 2019)** 2012–2023
  - category concentration: Solar PV R&amp;D (50% / 3.9×), Solar energy (50% / 2.4×)
  - lead orgs: University of NSW (70% / 44.9×), University of New South Wales (24% / 6.8×)
  - programmes: Australian Solar Institute (70%), Advancing Renewables Program (24%)
  - top projects: Australian Centre for Advanced Photovoltaics (ACAP) (207), Advanced High-Efficiency Silicon Solar Cells (71), Next Generation Silicon Solar Cells Above 26% Efficiency in Mass Production (5)

#### AMI — *Advanced Metering Infrastructure*

**technology** · 465 mentions / 64 docs

A system of smart meters, communication networks, and data management systems that enables two-way communication between utilities and customers, supporting time-varying tariffs, DER monitoring, and automated meter reading.

*Context (model):* Referenced in ARENA smart metering, demand management, and DER integration projects; mandatory AMI rollout in Victoria from 2009 provides a key case study.

*Fingerprint* (465 mentions / 64 docs):
  - trajectory: **steady (median 2020)** 2014–2025
  - category concentration: Distributed energy resources (61% / 7.8×), System security and reliability (10% / 2.8×), Electric vehicles (10% / 2.0×)
  - lead orgs: Jemena Electricity Networks (12% / 49.1×), Horizon Power (12% / 24.6×), Electricity Networks Corporation (Western Power) (28% / 17.3×)
  - top projects: Western Australia Distributed Energy Resources Orchestration Pilot (Project Symphony) (117), Horizon Power Business Model Pilot Phase 1 (51), Jemena Dynamic Electric Vehicle Charging Trial (51)

#### API — *Application Programming Interface*

**technology** · 2,550 mentions / 228 docs

A defined set of protocols and tools that allows software systems to communicate with each other. In energy contexts, APIs enable data exchange between DER devices, energy management systems, grid operators, and market platforms.

*Context (model):* Appears in ARENA DER integration, smart metering, and digital platform projects where interoperability between systems is a key design consideration.

*Fingerprint* (2,515 mentions / 228 docs):
  - trajectory: **steady (median 2019)** 2010–2025
  - category concentration: Distributed energy resources (35% / 4.5×), Electric vehicles (12% / 2.3×), Solar energy (13% / 0.6×)
  - lead orgs: AGL Energy Services (10% / 30.4×), SA Power Networks (16% / 15.0×)
  - top projects: Advanced VPP Grid Integration (301), AGL Electric Vehicle Orchestration Trial (212), Western Australia Distributed Energy Resources Orchestration Pilot (Project Symphony) (152)

#### AREMI — *Australian Renewable Energy Mapping Infrastructure*

**technology** · 352 mentions / 29 docs

An ARENA-developed online geospatial platform that provides publicly accessible maps of Australia's renewable energy resources, existing infrastructure, and planning information to support project development and research.

*Context (model):* Developed with ARENA funding and maintained as a public knowledge tool; referenced in ARENA project site selection and resource assessment documentation.

**technology** · 738 mentions / 48 docs

AEMO's operational solar energy forecasting system, which provides short-term and medium-term forecasts of aggregate solar PV generation across the NEM to support dispatch and system security management.

#### ASU — *Air Separation Unit*

**technology** · 219 mentions / 18 docs

An industrial plant that separates atmospheric air into its component gases — primarily nitrogen, oxygen, and argon — using cryogenic distillation. ASUs produce the oxygen required for various industrial processes and are energy-intensive, making them targets for flexible demand management powered by renewable energy.

*Context (model):* Appears in ARENA green hydrogen, industrial decarbonisation, and flexible demand project documentation.

*Fingerprint* (219 mentions / 18 docs):
  - trajectory: **falling (median 2012 vs corpus 2019)** 2012–2023
  - category concentration: Solar PV R&amp;D (46% / 3.6×), Solar energy (46% / 2.2×)
  - lead orgs: University of NSW (85% / 55.1×)
  - programmes: Australian Solar Institute (84%), Advancing Renewables Program (14%)
  - top projects: Australian Centre for Advanced Photovoltaics (ACAP) (184), ATCO - Scale H2 Feasibility Study (17), Gibson Island Renewable Ammonia Project FEED Study (7)

#### Battery Energy Storage System — *BESS*

**technology** · 416 mentions / 122 docs

A system of electrochemical battery cells used to store and dispatch electrical energy; deployed at utility, commercial, and residential scale.

*Context (model):* Appears across a large share of ARENA project documents covering storage trials, grid services, and VPP demonstrations.

#### BES — *Battery Energy Storage*

**technology** · 605 mentions / 7 docs

The storage of electrical energy using battery technology. BES is sometimes used as a shorter form of BESS; it encompasses the core battery technology component rather than the full system.

*Context (model):* Appears in ARENA storage project documentation, sometimes interchangeably with BESS.

*Fingerprint* (605 mentions / 7 docs):
  - trajectory: **steady (median 2019)** 2014–2022

  - top projects: Advanced Planning of PV-Rich Distribution Networks Study (535), Energy Storage for Commercial Renewable Integration (60), MGA Thermal Energy Storage Project (2)

#### BESS — *Battery Energy Storage System*

**technology** · 7,696 mentions / 244 docs

A complete system for storing and releasing electrical energy using batteries, comprising cells, a battery management system (BMS), power conversion equipment, and balance-of-system components. Deployed at scales from residential to grid-scale (hundreds of MW).

*Context (model):* Central to many ARENA grid-stability, renewable integration, and storage projects; 'BESS IES' and 'BESS BoS' variants refer to integrated energy systems and balance-of-system configurations respectively.

*Fingerprint* (7,684 mentions / 244 docs):
  - trajectory: **steady (median 2018)** 2012–2025
  - category concentration: Battery storage (40% / 4.5×), Solar energy (15% / 0.7×)
  - lead orgs: ElectraNet Pty Limited (12% / 30.8×), Infigen Energy (11% / 27.3×)
  - top projects: Energy Storage for Commercial Renewable Integration (ESCRI) Phase 2 (837), Lake Bonney Battery Energy Storage System (743), NT Solar Energy Transformation Program (571)

#### BEV — *Battery Electric Vehicle*

**technology** · 659 mentions / 57 docs

A vehicle powered entirely by an electric motor drawing energy from an on-board battery pack, with no internal combustion engine. BEVs are recharged from the electricity grid and are the primary focus of vehicle-to-grid (V2G) and smart charging research.

*Context (model):* Appears in ARENA EV charging, V2G, and transport decarbonisation project documentation.

*Fingerprint* (633 mentions / 57 docs):
  - trajectory: **rising (median 2022 vs corpus 2019)** 2018–2025
  - category concentration: Electric vehicles (95% / 17.9×)
  - lead orgs: Team Global Express Pty Ltd (formerly IPEC Pty) (34% / 103.5×), Patrick Stevedores Operations No 2 Pty Ltd (Patrick Terminals) (24% / 101.2×), Toll Transport Pty. Ltd. (10% / 64.0×)
  - programmes: Future Fuels Program (34%), Driving the Nation Program (43%)
  - top projects: Depot of the Future Vehicle Electrification Project (165), Patrick Terminals - BEV Terminal Trucks at Fremantle Port (121), Toll Group - Project Truckvolt (51)

#### BF — *Blast Furnace*

**technology** · 654 mentions / 27 docs

A large industrial furnace used to smelt iron ore by reducing it with coke (carbon) and air, producing pig iron as a precursor to steel. Blast furnaces are carbon-intensive and are a major decarbonisation challenge targeted by green hydrogen DRI pathways.

*Context (model):* Referenced in ARENA green hydrogen and industrial decarbonisation projects examining steel industry emissions reduction.

*Fingerprint* (654 mentions / 27 docs):
  - trajectory: **rising (median 2022 vs corpus 2019)** 2012–2024
  - category concentration: Hydrogen energy (79% / 10.6×), Renewables for industry (19% / 3.5×)
  - lead orgs: Bluescope Steel (79% / 139.0×)
  - top projects: Port Kembla Steelworks Renewables & Emissions Reduction Study (502), UNSW - blast furnace innovations: sustainable, low-carbon ironmaking project (55), Industry ETI Establishment Phase (27)

#### BSF — *Back Surface Field*

**technology** · 156 mentions / 18 docs

A doping region on the rear of a conventional solar cell that creates an electric field to repel minority carriers and reduce rear-surface recombination, improving cell efficiency. BSF has largely been superseded by passivated rear-contact designs (PERC).

*Context (model):* Appears in ARENA solar cell research and technology evolution documentation.

*Fingerprint* (156 mentions / 18 docs):
  - trajectory: **falling (median 2012 vs corpus 2019)** 2010–2023
  - category concentration: Large-scale solar (15% / 5.6×), Solar PV R&amp;D (34% / 2.7×), Solar energy (50% / 2.4×)
  - lead orgs: Elecnor Australia Pty Ltd (30% / 184.4×), University of NSW (46% / 29.6×), University of NSW (UNSW) (13% / 20.1×)
  - programmes: Regional Australia’s Renewables (30%), Australian Solar Institute (59%)
  - top projects: Australian Centre for Advanced Photovoltaics (ACAP) (35), Barcaldine Remote Community Solar Farm (23), Overcoming Performance Limitations of Commercial Solar Cells (10)

#### CCGT — *Combined Cycle Gas Turbine*

**technology** · 421 mentions / 25 docs

A power plant that uses both a gas turbine and a steam turbine in sequence to generate electricity from natural gas, achieving higher efficiency (typically 55–60%) than an open cycle gas turbine (OCGT) alone.

*Context (model):* Referenced in ARENA system planning, firming capacity, and energy transition documents as a dispatchable generation technology being compared with or complemented by renewables and storage.

*Fingerprint* (386 mentions / 25 docs):
  - trajectory: **falling (median 2012 vs corpus 2019)** 2012–2024
  - category concentration: Hybrid technologies (59% / 17.9×), Hydropower / Pumped Hydro Energy Storage (25% / 9.1×)
  - lead orgs: Snowy Hydro (18% / 222.9×), University of Melbourne (59% / 38.4×)
  - top projects: Achieving Cost-Affective Abatement (154), Snowy 2.0 Feasibility Study (47), Atlas of Pumped Hydro Energy Storage (9)

*Notes:* The variant 'CGT' likely refers to the same technology or is a data artefact.

#### CCS — *Carbon Capture and Storage*

**technology** · 848 mentions / 55 docs

A process that captures CO₂ emissions from large point sources (such as power stations or industrial facilities), compresses the gas, and stores it permanently in geological formations. CCS is considered a potential decarbonisation pathway for hard-to-abate industries.

*Context (model):* Referenced in ARENA low-emissions technology roadmap documents and comparisons with renewable alternatives for industrial decarbonisation.

*Fingerprint* (848 mentions / 55 docs):
  - trajectory: **steady (median 2020)** 2012–2024
  - category concentration: Hybrid technologies (27% / 8.4×), Renewables for industry (37% / 7.0×)
  - lead orgs: Monash University (ClimateWorks Australia) (41% / 127.0×), University of Melbourne (31% / 20.0×)
  - top projects: Achieving Cost-Affective Abatement (206), Australian Industry ETI Delivery Stage Project (171), Industry ETI Establishment Phase (107)

#### CHP — *Combined Heat and Power*

**technology** · 314 mentions / 33 docs

Also known as cogeneration, CHP simultaneously generates electricity and useful heat from a single fuel source, significantly improving overall energy efficiency compared with separate generation of heat and power.

*Context (model):* Appears in ARENA bioenergy, industrial energy efficiency, and gas project documentation where simultaneous heat and power production improves project economics.

*Fingerprint* (314 mentions / 33 docs):
  - trajectory: **rising (median 2022 vs corpus 2019)** 2012–2025
  - category concentration: Geothermal energy (18% / 21.0×), Renewables for industry (33% / 6.1×), Hydrogen energy (15% / 2.0×)

  - top projects: Yolarno - Feasibility Study into Resource Recovery Facility (26), MGA Thermal Energy Storage Project (17), Australian Hydrogen Centre (10)

A compound semiconductor thin-film material used as the absorber layer in high-efficiency thin-film solar cells. CIGS cells offer competitive efficiencies and can be manufactured on flexible substrates.

*Context (model):* Appears in ARENA next-generation and thin-film solar cell research project documentation.

*Fingerprint* (531 mentions / 31 docs):

#### EMT — *Electromagnetic Transient*

**technology** · 151 mentions / 19 docs

A class of power system simulation that models the fast electrical dynamics of power systems (microseconds to milliseconds) including switching transients, harmonics, and sub-cycle phenomena. EMT simulations are increasingly required for studying high IBR penetration scenarios.

*Context (model):* Referenced in ARENA grid stability, IBR integration, and system strength project documentation where detailed dynamic modelling of inverter behaviour is required.

*Fingerprint* (151 mentions / 19 docs):
  - trajectory: **rising (median 2023 vs corpus 2019)** 2016–2024
  - category concentration: System security and reliability (86% / 22.1×), Battery storage (11% / 1.3×)
  - lead orgs: Powerlink (16% / 68.2×), Monash University (55% / 52.5×), AEMO (10% / 42.6×)
  - top projects: Monash Grid Oscillation Project (Study and Software Development) (71), Powerlink Cost-Effective System Strength Study (24), AEMO Connections Tool Project (15)

#### GFL — *Grid-Following*

**technology** · 503 mentions / 21 docs

A control mode for inverter-based resources in which the inverter synchronises to and follows the existing grid voltage and frequency reference rather than establishing its own. Most current solar and wind inverters operate in grid-following mode.

*Context (model):* Contrasted with grid-forming (GFM) inverters in ARENA grid stability and IBR research projects.

*Fingerprint* (503 mentions / 21 docs):
  - trajectory: **rising (median 2022 vs corpus 2019)** 2019–2024
  - category concentration: System security and reliability (31% / 8.1×), Distributed energy resources (31% / 4.0×), Battery storage (35% / 3.9×)
  - lead orgs: The University of Tasmania (43% / 264.1×), Victorian Big Battery HoldCo Pty Ltd (ABN 73 644 551 940) (21% / 66.4×), Victorian Big Battery HoldCo Pty Ltd (13% / 28.1×)
  - top projects: Optimal DER Scheduling for Frequency Stability (185), Neoen Big Battery (Blyth) Deployment Project (93), Neoen Big Battery (Western Downs) Deployment Project (55)

#### GFM — *Grid-Forming*

**technology** · 1,427 mentions / 28 docs

A control mode for inverter-based resources (such as batteries and solar PV) in which the inverter actively establishes voltage and frequency references, behaving like a synchronous generator. Grid-forming inverters can support power system stability in low-inertia grids with high renewable penetration.

*Context (model):* A key theme in ARENA grid stability and inverter research projects, particularly those examining the transition to a high-IBR NEM where traditional synchronous inertia is declining.

*Fingerprint* (1,427 mentions / 28 docs):
  - trajectory: **rising (median 2023 vs corpus 2019)** 2019–2024
  - category concentration: System security and reliability (33% / 8.5×), Battery storage (54% / 6.1×), Distributed energy resources (11% / 1.4×)
  - lead orgs: The University of Tasmania (16% / 100.0×), Victorian Big Battery HoldCo Pty Ltd (ABN 73 644 551 940) (30% / 93.5×), Victorian Big Battery HoldCo Pty Ltd (38% / 77.7×)
  - top projects: Neoen Big Battery (Blyth) Deployment Project (318), Neoen Victorian Big Battery (Moorabool) Retrofit (222), Neoen Big Battery (Western Downs) Deployment Project (174)

*Notes:* Contrasts with 'grid-following' (GFL) inverter control, which tracks an existing grid voltage reference.

A unique alphanumeric string assigned to a digital publication (journal article, report, dataset) to provide a permanent, reliable link to the content. DOIs are used as standard citation identifiers in scientific publications.

*Context (model):* Appears in ARENA research project publication lists and reference sections as a citation standard.

*Fingerprint* (269 mentions / 49 docs):
  - trajectory: **falling (median 2012 vs corpus 2019)** 2009–2024
  - category concentration: Solar PV R&amp;D (45% / 3.5×), Solar energy (45% / 2.2×)
  - lead orgs: University of NSW (66% / 42.8×)
  - programmes: Australian Solar Institute (68%), Advancing Renewables Program (14%)
  - top projects: Australian Centre for Advanced Photovoltaics (ACAP) (172), Creating a Better Solar Cell (12), Improving Efficiency, Durability & Cost-effectiveness of III-V Semiconductors (10)

#### DR — *Demand Response*

**concept** · 3,021 mentions / 129 docs

The deliberate adjustment of electricity consumption by end-users in response to price signals, grid operator instructions, or automated controls, to help balance supply and demand. DR can reduce peak demand, support frequency control, and defer network augmentation.

*Context (model):* Featured in ARENA demand management, DER orchestration, and market reform projects; analysts should note that 'Dr' as a title variant is noise.

*Fingerprint* (3,017 mentions / 129 docs):
  - trajectory: **falling (median 2017 vs corpus 2019)** 2010–2024
  - category concentration: Demand response (79% / 19.8×)
  - lead orgs: Zen Ecosystems Pty Ltd. (20% / 62.2×), Enel X Australia Pty Ltd (24% / 42.4×), AGL Energy Services Pty Ltd (12% / 30.5×)
  - top projects: Enel X Demand Response Project (429), Zen Ecosystems Demand Response (360), AGL Demand Response (221)

*Notes:* The variant 'Dr' is a personal title and should be treated as noise in glossary contexts.

#### DSM — *Demand Side Management*

**concept** · 305 mentions / 21 docs

A broad set of policies, programmes, and technologies aimed at modifying consumer electricity demand patterns to improve efficiency, reduce peak demand, and lower system costs. DSM encompasses energy efficiency, demand response, and load shifting.

*Context (model):* Appears in ARENA demand management, network alternatives, and DER integration project documentation.

*Fingerprint* (298 mentions / 21 docs):
  - trajectory: **falling (median 2014 vs corpus 2019)** 2012–2023

#### DSO — *Distribution System Operator*

**concept** · 3,698 mentions / 80 docs

A proposed or emerging operational role in which a distribution-level entity actively manages power flows, DER dispatch, and local network constraints — extending beyond the traditional asset-owner function of a DNSP. The DSO model is under policy consideration in the NEM.

*Context (model):* Referenced in ARENA future-grid and DER integration projects exploring market structures and operational models that could enable greater DER participation.

*Fingerprint* (3,624 mentions / 80 docs):
  - trajectory: **rising (median 2021 vs corpus 2019)** 2015–2025
  - category concentration: Distributed energy resources (82% / 10.4×)
  - lead orgs: Electricity Networks Corporation (Western Power) (71% / 43.8×)
  - top projects: Western Australia Distributed Energy Resources Orchestration Pilot (Project Symphony) (2171), AEMO - Project EDGE (Energy Demand and Generation Exchange) (269), Project Jupiter (263)

*Notes:* The DSO role is not yet formally established in the NEM; its definition and governance are still subject to regulatory reform discussions.

The electrochemical reaction at the anode of a water electrolysis cell in which water molecules are oxidised to produce oxygen gas and protons (or hydroxide ions). OER is typically the kinetically limiting half-reaction in water electrolysis.

*Context (model):* Appears in ARENA electrolyser materials and green hydrogen research project documentation examining catalyst performance.

*Fingerprint* (166 mentions / 12 docs):
  - trajectory: **steady (median 2018)** 2018–2024
  - category concentration: Hydrogen energy (85% / 11.3×), Renewables for industry (14% / 2.7×)
  - lead orgs: MIH2 Pty Ltd (21% / 130.9×), MIH2 (14% / 44.9×), University of New South Wales (54% / 15.0×)
  - top projects: Highly Efficient & Low Cost Photovoltaic-Electrolysis System (81), Scale up & demonstration of next-generation CSIRO axial flow electrolyser (35), Fortescue - low temperature direct electrochemical reduction for zero emissions iron (24)

#### ROCOF — *Rate of Change of Frequency*

**concept** · 190 mentions / 31 docs

#### NEM — *National Electricity Market*

*Fingerprint* (14,325 mentions / 546 docs):
